# Skills · P · part 151

829 entries, prototype to prove-it-works. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **prototype**: Asks a few questions, writes a one-page brief, builds a playable browser game as one HTML folder it plays and screenshots; with an engine set, a throwaway engine or paper prototype. Ends on proceed, pivot or kill. Use when the user says make me a game, wants to try a game idea or mechanic, needs a quick spike, asks to change a game it made, or says they love or hate it. ([Donchitos/Claude-Code-Game-Studios](https://github.com/Donchitos/Claude-Code-Game-Studios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prototype) · 690 installs · 13 copies)
- **prototype**: Builds throwaway code that answers one design question: a single-file HTML demo to push a state model or business logic through hard cases, or several structurally different UI variants on one route, switchable in the browser. Use when the user wants to check whether logic or a state model feels right, explore what a screen should look like before committing, or settle a wayfinder prototype ticket with something runnable. ([dougborg/harness-kit](https://github.com/dougborg/harness-kit/tree/HEAD/claude-skills/prototype) · 2 copies)
- **prototype**: Throwaway code that answers one design question: a logic model, UI variants, or a script that settles an empirical fork. Use when talking cannot settle a decision but running something can. ([duy-tung/tstack](https://github.com/duy-tung/tstack/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: The single prototyping skill. Frame the intent and pick the output mode up front (an HTML sketch, Figma screens, or iso-prod in the real codebase), then use THIS repo's real design system as the source of truth (it discovers the stack, tokens, components, conventions), propose options, and build with UI/UX rigor (4px grid, component consistency, information hierarchy). Use whenever the user wants to prototype, mock up, design screens, "show options for X", "build a clickable demo", or build a feature/flow as a throwaway to evaluate. Frontend-only, mock data, no prod. Works in any repo. ([eFounders/design-skills](https://github.com/eFounders/design-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Build a throwaway prototype to answer a design question — sanity-check a data model, exercise a state machine, or compare UI variations on one route. Use when the user wants to mock up options, try a few designs, or 'let me play with it' before committing. For a brief without code use `shape`; for vague intent use `brainstorming`. ([ekasc/skills](https://github.com/ekasc/skills/tree/HEAD/prototype))
- **prototype**: Build a throwaway prototype to answer a product or design question fast, skipping the normal Agent OS process (no slice, stories, standards or tests first). - **prototype**: Routes a design into the project's design pipeline and lists what a build needs that the design doesn't have yet; writes nothing. Use when asked how to get a design ready to build from. ([lukekeith/builder](https://github.com/lukekeith/builder/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Build a throwaway artifact to answer "how should it look/behave?" Use when a wayfinder ticket needs a concrete artifact to react to before a decision can be made. Two modes — logic (terminal) or UI (browser). ([lynchypin/project-onboard](https://github.com/lynchypin/project-onboard/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: 做一個用完即丟的原型（prototype）來回答設計問題。使用者想確認某個狀態模型或邏輯感覺對不對，或想探索某個 UI 該長什麼樣子時使用。 ([lystyp/useful-skill](https://github.com/lystyp/useful-skill/tree/HEAD/mattpocock-skills-zh/skills/engineering/prototype))
- **prototype**: Phase 2 of the prototyping workflow. Build the idea directly and fast — implement in the current workspace with NO TDD, no subagent dispatch, and no code-review gate. Prioritizes a working prototype to look at over correctness guarantees. Hands off to run-locally. ([mac-builds-things/AI-Toolbox](https://github.com/mac-builds-things/AI-Toolbox/tree/HEAD/plugins/workflows/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Builds an isolated disposable experiment that answers one design question. Use when code or a visible UI comparison is cheaper and more reliable than further discussion, and the result must not enter production by accident. ([mad818/personal](https://github.com/mad818/personal/tree/HEAD/docs/ideas/skills/mattpocock-engineering/prototype))
- **prototype**: Use when exploring how a feature or screen could work before committing to a direction — "prototype this", "let's prototype", "show me options", "what could this look like", "mock it up". Builds the brief from the conversation so far (or interviews the user when there isn't one), then generates several low-fidelity, interactive HTML wireframes in a throwaway folder, recommends one, and iterates on the user's pick. Fast, cheap, disposable — a thinking tool, not finished work. ([madebydiego/prototype](https://github.com/madebydiego/prototype/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Use when trying something out before it's built for real. ([MalasataXD/unfuss](https://github.com/MalasataXD/unfuss/tree/HEAD/skills/understand/prototype))
- **prototype**: Build a throwaway prototype to answer a design question. Use when the brief or coordinator asks to sanity-check whether a state model or logic feels right, or explore what a UI should look like. ([maou-shonen/skills](https://github.com/maou-shonen/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/matt/prototype))
- **prototype**: Use when a disposable experiment can answer a bounded design, logic, usability, or technical feasibility question. ([mascah/skills](https://github.com/mascah/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Build throwaway code to answer design, architecture, flow, or interaction questions before production implementation. Use for spikes, POCs, mockups, quick versions, comparing options, or uncertainty that running code can resolve. Covers isolated greenfield throwaways and brownfield prototypes placed behind one replaceable seam. ([MatanelGordon/skills](https://github.com/MatanelGordon/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Construa um protótipo descartável para responder a uma dúvida de design. Use quando o usuário quiser verificar se um modelo de estado ou lógica faz sentido, ou explorar como uma interface deve parecer. ([matheusdmlopes/skills-ptbr](https://github.com/matheusdmlopes/skills-ptbr/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/prototype))
- **prototype**: Disciplined throwaway prototyping. Two distinct branches — terminal harness for "does this state model feel right" and URL-switchable UI variants for "what should this look like". Hard rule: capture the verdict, then delete. Files are named with `prototype.` prefix so they cannot rot into production silently. ([mattyatplay-coder/tetra](https://github.com/mattyatplay-coder/tetra/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prototype) · 2 copies)
- **prototype**: Build a throwaway prototype to settle a design question or an empirical fork by observing it, instead of asking a question a quick sketch could answer. Use for prototype, mock it up, sketch this, try this layout, or when checking whether a state model or logic feels right. ([maveeshah/my-skills](https://github.com/maveeshah/my-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Build throwaway prototypes. Duplicate existing components, wire to variant switcher. ([Max-Zhuuu/waypoint-skills](https://github.com/Max-Zhuuu/waypoint-skills/tree/HEAD/design/prototype))
- **prototype**: Interactively craft a feature's UI mockup as self-contained drivable HTML anchored to the real frontend. Use on /afk:prototype after the PRD exists and before the SDD locks decisions. ([midnightblur/afk-driver](https://github.com/midnightblur/afk-driver/tree/HEAD/skills/afk/prototype))
- **prototype**: Build a throwaway prototype to answer one design question — a runnable terminal app for state/logic questions, or several radically different UI variations toggleable from one route. Use when the user wants to sanity-check whether a state model or logic feels right, or explore what a UI should look like. ([MinhDuyDEV/mdroid](https://github.com/MinhDuyDEV/mdroid/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: 設計確定前に使い捨て prototype で状態・ロジック（terminal）や UI 案（1 route 切替）を比較するときに使う。 ([Miraius-LLC/worksmobile-message-bot](https://github.com/Miraius-LLC/worksmobile-message-bot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Codex throwaway prototype workflow：建立小型 disposable experiments，回答具體的 design、state 或 UI question。Miyago 要 prototype、sanity-check model、嘗試 UI options、探索 state machine，或在 production code 前快速試作時使用。 ([miyago9267/dotfile](https://github.com/miyago9267/dotfile/tree/HEAD/config/ai/codex/skills/prototype) · 2 copies)
- **prototype**: 設計を確定する前に、使い捨てのプロトタイプを作って設計を具体化する。2つの分岐を振り分ける — 状態・ビジネスロジックの検証には実行可能なターミナルアプリ、UI検討には1つのルートで切り替えられる複数の大きく異なるUIバリエーション。プロトタイプを作りたい、データモデルや状態機械を確認したい、UIをモックアップしたい、設計オプションを検討したい、あるいは「prototype this」「let me play with it」「try a few designs」「プロトタイプして」「ちょっと触ってみたい」「いくつか案を試したい」と言ったときに使う。 ([mizunomi32/skills](https://github.com/mizunomi32/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/prototype))
- **prototype**: Scaffold a throwaway SwiftUI prototype to validate a concept or interaction. ([mkaya93/Claude-Code-iOS-Studios](https://github.com/mkaya93/Claude-Code-iOS-Studios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Run a throwaway engineering spike to retire one important uncertainty and return evidence to a plan or decision map. Use for UI, state, algorithm, API, data model, integration, AI, performance, or migration questions. ([Mohammed-Moniem/codex-engineering-system](https://github.com/Mohammed-Moniem/codex-engineering-system/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Use when building the first working version of something real — a prototype, MVP, or proof-of-concept — quickly, but as a sound and extensible base rather than a throwaway. Covers timed or scoped builds like hackathons and take-homes. Not for hardening an established system or pure problem-space exploration. ([moiri-gamboni/praxis](https://github.com/moiri-gamboni/praxis/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Loaded by other skills to show the user a screen element as a working HTML prototype and iterate it with them, round by round, until it matches what they want. Carries how the file is built, how states and interactions are made switchable, how alternatives are offered and how corrections are applied. The loading skill says what the prototype is of and which decisions it stands for. ([MolinaFranco/claude-tools](https://github.com/MolinaFranco/claude-tools/tree/HEAD/mahou/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Use during research-slice to build disposable experiments that answer technical uncertainty. ([MonsieurBarti/tff-mono](https://github.com/MonsieurBarti/tff-mono/tree/HEAD/packages/core/src/content/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Throwaway prototype that answers one design or feel question: a single-file demo of a state model, or several structurally different UI variants. Never ships. Not for comparing whole builds, UX planning, real shippable UI, open design dialogue, user testing sessions, or loop prompts. ([Motaghi/mimir-skills](https://github.com/Motaghi/mimir-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/prototype))
- **prototype**: Taahhüt öncesi tasarımı netleştirmek için kullanılıp atılacak prototip oluşturur; terminal uygulaması veya UI varyasyonları arasında yönlendirir. ([Mutfak-Yazilimevi/.github](https://github.com/Mutfak-Yazilimevi/.github/tree/HEAD/project-template/.claude/skills/dev-prototype))

([MVPavan/coding-ritual](https://github.com/MVPavan/coding-ritual/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prototype))

- **protracted-strategy**: 持久战略：面对长期、复杂、短期不可能速胜的任务，客观评估态势，划分战略防御 / 相持 / 反攻三阶段，判定当前所处阶段，设置阶段转换条件，并在困难阶段仍给出局部进攻点。当任务跨多个会话或多轮迭代、资源暂时不足、或出现"想放弃"与"一把梭"两种情绪时触发；任务能在当前会话完成时不触发。 English: Protracted strategy. For long-horizon work that cannot be won quickly, assess the situation objectively, divide the effort into defense / stalemate / counter-offensive stages, name the current stage and its exit condition, and always identify a local offensive even while accumulating.
